Fissidens asplenioides · 162997

PASS

A usually robust moss of unspecified native origin, growing on moist sandstone ledges and in crevices near streams and waterfalls. Its leaves typically curl tightly inward from their tips when dry, and subterranean reproductive bodies have been reported specifically from Macaronesian specimens.
